- **macro-release-repricing**: Use when running a prediction-market agent on scheduled macro releases — FOMC decisions, CPI and inflation prints, jobs reports and the rate path they imply — where the event time is known in advance, a professional market prices the same question first, and the edge lives in the hours around the print rather than in a forecast. Covers how to read the external market as a prior, why the pre-print hours are usually a trap, and what actually mispriced looks like here.
